### [128] [Characterisation of a multistable turbulent wake: application of an improved regime identification with analytical model training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23206)
*Ariane Barlet,Pierre Bragança,Christophe Cuvier,Joran Rolland*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过实验和建模研究了两个并列方形杆（间距G/H变化）在雷诺数10000下尾流的多稳态射流现象。使用PIV测量速度场，以射流加权横向位置Y_m和射流宽度w为特征量，发现三种多稳态模式：三稳态（G/H∈[1.15,1.25]）、双稳态（G/H∈[1.5,2.65]）和单稳态（G/H∈[3.0,3.5]）。通过数据驱动的随机微分方程（SDE）建模Y_m，采用多项式漂移和扩散项，在不同稳态区域恢复理论分岔漂移，并对复杂多稳态区间的非典型漂移进行拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过实验和建模系统研究并列方形杆尾流中射流的多稳态行为，理解不同间距比下的稳态转变机制，特别是从双稳态到单稳态的过渡以及复杂多稳态现象。

Method: 在风洞中进行实验，使用二维二分量PIV测量尾流速度场；定义射流加权横向位置Y_m和射流宽度w作为特征量；采用数据驱动的随机微分方程（SDE）方法，以多项式漂移和扩散项对Y_m进行建模，通过权衡模型简洁性与精度来拟合参数。

Result: 三稳态出现在G/H∈[1.15,1.25]，双稳态在G/H∈[1.5,2.65]，单稳态在G/H∈[3.0,3.5]；在前两个区间内存在更复杂的多稳态行为；SDE模型在简单稳态下恢复理论分岔漂移，同时修正了乘法噪声；在复杂多稳态区间拟合出非典型漂移表达式。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的SDE建模方法能有效描述射流多稳态行为，既能恢复理论分岔漂移并引入合适的噪声项，也能处理复杂多稳态区域中的非典型行为，为理解射流多稳态机制提供了有力工具。

Abstract: This article presents the experimental study and the modelling of the multistable jet in the wake of two side by side square bars separated by a distance $G$ at Reynolds number $R=U_\infty H/ν=10000$ (with $U_\infty$ the velocity of the incoming flow, $H$ the bar side and $ν$ the kinematic viscosity). The velocity field downstream of the bars is measured by means of two dimensional two components Particle Image Velocimetry (PIV). We use the weighted transverse position of the jet $Y_m$ and the jet width $w$ to characterise the regimes of multistability as the gap ratio $G/H$ is increased. Three main regimes of multistability are possible: tristability, bistability, and monostability. In our wind tunnel, tristability is observed for $G/H\in [1.15,1.25]$, bistability is observed for $G/H\in [1.5,2.65]$ and monostability is observed for $G/H\in [3.0,3.5]$. Within the first two ranges of $G/H$, there exists gap ratios for which multistability is more complex. In order to analyse the simple and complex multistability regimes as well as the transition from bistable to monostable, we construct an analytical stochastic differential equation (SDE) modelling $Y_m$ for each gap ratio. These SDEs are written with polynomial drift and diffusion. For this matter we use a data based method that finds an trade--off between simplicity of the model (smaller number of monomials) and precision. A first key advantage of the use of the data-based model fitting method is that when the flow is tristable, bistable or monostable, we recover the drifts expected from the theory of bifurcations, but we are now able to correct it with the right multiplicative noise expressed by the diffusion. The second key advantage is that we can also fit atypical drift expressions when the multistability regime is complex that help us make sense of the jet behaviour.

</details>


### [129] [Dynamics of fluid-fluid displacements in a model rough fracture beyond the quasistatic limit: A spectral approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23759)
*Mykyta Chubynsky,Jordi Ortin,Marco Dentz,Ran Holtzman*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种谱计算方法，用于研究多孔介质中流体-流体驱替过程的界面演化和能量耗散，揭示了微观Haines跳跃与宏观滞后现象之间的联系，并证明粘性力仅影响弛豫时间而不改变总耗散量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔介质中流体驱替的微观Haines跳跃与宏观能量耗散及滞后现象密切相关，但微观与宏观尺度之间的定量联系以及介质固有非均匀性的影响尚不明确，现有模拟方法计算成本过高。

Method: 开发了一种谱计算方法，模拟可变孔径Hele-Shaw细胞中的界面演化与能量耗散，通过与CFD模拟和实验验证，在单缺陷和随机粗糙度两种场景下进行测试。

Result: 该方法在不同尺度下均能高效捕捉非平凡动力学行为；理论上证明粘性力在Haines跳跃期间完全解释了连续亚稳态平衡之间的能量耗散，但粘性不改变总耗散量，仅影响弛豫时间。

Conclusion: 该谱计算方法为多孔介质中流体-流体流动的尺度提升提供了基础，有助于理解控制模式与速率对滞后和耗散的影响。

Abstract: In fluid-fluid displacements in porous and fractured materials, viscous friction in microscale interfacial (Haines) jumps is intimately linked to macroscale energy dissipation and the associated pressure-saturation (retention) hysteresis. The mode of control (flow rate vs. pressure) and the driving rate (from quasistatic to finite) can substantially affect hysteresis. Despite the significance of hysteresis and dissipation in various technological and natural processes, a quantitative understanding of the link between the micro- and macro-scales, and of the impact of the inherent heterogeneity of porous media, remains elusive. An ``imperfect'' Hele-Shaw cell of variable aperture is a simple model system which allows to study all these in details. However, simulating fluid-fluid interface evolution in heterogeneous media, even in such a simple system, is computationally prohibitive, as multiple length and time scales need to be resolved simultaneously. We develop here a spectral computational approach for interface evolution and energy dissipation and validate it via comparison to computational fluid dynamics simulations and experiments. Computational efficiency is demonstrated by following interface evolution in a cell with a single ``defect'', as well as with random roughness; in both, disparate length scales lead to nontrivial dynamics over many orders of magnitude in time. We also show theoretically that while viscous forces during Haines jumps fully account for the energy dissipated between consecutive metastable equilibria, viscosity does not change the total dissipated amount, merely the relaxation time. Our approach provides a stepping stone towards upscaling of fluid-fluid flows in porous media.

</details>


### [130] [Adjoint Sensitivity Maps for Passive Flow Control Around Rotating Circular Cylinders Across a Wide Operating Envelope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23831)
*Niklas Kühl*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文采用基于拓扑的伴随敏感性分析方法，对旋转圆柱在雷诺数1E+01至1E+07、自旋比0至2π范围内的阻力和升力敏感性进行了系统研究，生成了敏感性图谱，为被动流动控制设计提供了直观指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转圆柱（如Flettner转子）在工程中广泛应用，除优化圆柱自身气动性能外，在其附近布置被动流动控制装置有望进一步调控气动力，因此需要系统的方法来识别敏感区域以指导设计。

Method: 采用基于拓扑的伴随敏感性分析方法，利用多孔介质公式推导阻力、升力和扭矩的局部敏感性场，并通过分布式Darcy型源项和敏感性驱动的被动流动控制结构进行正向仿真验证。

Result: 敏感性场的大尺度拓扑主要由自旋比主导，雷诺数在大部分研究范围内的影响相对较弱；生成了敏感性图谱，展示了阻力和升力敏感性联合符号分布，可直接识别局部动量提取对两个目标同时改善或恶化的区域。

Conclusion: 该敏感性图谱为被动流动控制概念提供了实用设计指导，并表明在中等操作范围内可能存在鲁棒性解。

Abstract: Rotating circular cylinders are employed in a variety of engineering applications, one prominent example being Flettner rotors for wind-assisted ship propulsion. Besides optimizing the aerodynamic performance of the cylinder itself, passive flow-control devices placed in its vicinity offer additional potential for manipulating the resulting aerodynamic forces. The present work introduces a topology-based adjoint sensitivity analysis for rotating circular cylinders over a wide operating envelope covering Reynolds numbers from 1E+01 to 1E+07 and spinning ratios between 0 and 2 pi. Local sensitivity fields associated with drag, lift, and torque are derived using a porous-medium formulation and validated by dedicated forward simulations employing both distributed Darcy-type source terms and a sensitivity-informed passive flow-control structure. Particular emphasis is placed on the combined sign distribution of the drag and lift sensitivities, yielding intuitive design maps that directly identify regions where local momentum extraction simultaneously improves or deteriorates both objectives. A systematic investigation of the resulting sensitivity spectra reveals that the large-scale topology of the sensitivity fields is governed primarily by the spinning ratio, whereas the influence of the Reynolds number remains comparatively weak over large parts of the investigated operating envelope. The resulting sensitivity atlas provides practical design guidance for passive flow-control concepts and demonstrates that robust solutions may exist over moderate operating ranges.

</details>


### [131] [Influence of a vertical-wall leading edge on bouncing and escape bubble rising regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24248)
*A. Rubio-González,E. J. Vega,R. Bolaños-Jiménez*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了超纯水中可变形气泡在垂直壁面附近的上升行为，重点探讨壁面前缘位置对气泡近壁动力学的影响。实验设置两种构型：(i) 壁面从气泡注入点下方11–25倍气泡直径处起始，使气泡持续受边界影响；(ii) 壁面前缘位于注入点上方144 mm处（约80–180倍气泡直径），使气泡先达到终端速度再进入壁面区域。结果表明，壁面前缘位置影响周期性弹跳（PB）向弹跳-翻滚-逃逸（BTE）动力学的转变以及PB regime内的反弹频率。下游壁面构型中BTE在更小气泡尺寸（更低Bo、Ga、Re数）下发生。两种构型在低Bo数下Strouhal数（St）趋势相近，但在Bo≥0.15时出现分歧。PB regime下，从注入点延伸的壁面构型中St接近常值0.014，而下游壁面构型中St显著更低。研究发现气泡动力学不仅取决于传统控制参数和壁面间距，还受壁面几何形状及气泡预先的流体动力学历史影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究可变形气泡在垂直壁面附近上升时，壁面前缘位置（即气泡与壁面开始相互作用的起始点）如何影响其近壁动力学行为，特别是周期性弹跳（PB）与弹跳-翻滚-逃逸（BTE）两种动力学模式的转变条件及频率特性。

Method: 在超纯水中进行实验，设置两种壁面构型：持续受壁面影响的构型和气泡先达到终端速度再进入壁面区域的构型。通过改变气泡尺寸和壁面位置，测量BTE转变的临界Bo、Ga、Re数，以及PB regime下的Strouhal数（St）变化规律。

Result: 下游壁面构型中BTE在更小气泡尺寸（更低Bo、Ga、Re数）下发生；两种构型在低Bo数下St趋势相近，但Bo≥0.15时出现分歧；PB regime下从注入点延伸的壁面构型St≈0.014，下游壁面构型St显著更低；下游壁面构型中更大的反弹幅度和更长的返回阶段导致更低频率。

Conclusion: 气泡动力学不仅取决于传统控制参数（Bo、Ga、Re）和壁面间距，还受壁面几何形状及气泡预先的流体动力学历史（即气泡进入壁面区域前的运动状态）影响。

Abstract: This work investigates deformable gas bubbles rising near a vertical wall in ultrapure water, focusing on how the position of the wall leading edge affects their near-wall dynamics. Two configurations are considered: (i) a wall extending from 11--25 bubble diameters below the bubble injection point, so that the bubble rises under the continuous influence of the boundary; and (ii) a wall whose leading edge is located 144 mm above the injector, corresponding to approximately 80--180 bubble diameters, allowing the bubble to reach its terminal velocity before entering the wall-bounded region. The results show that the wall leading-edge position influences both the transition from periodic bouncing (PB) to bouncing--tumbling--escaping (BTE) dynamics and the rebound frequency within the PB regime. When the wall leading edge is placed downstream, the onset of BTE occurs at smaller bubble sizes, corresponding simultaneously to lower Bond ($Bo$), Galilei ($Ga$), and Reynolds ($Re$) numbers. The Strouhal number ($St$) follows a similar decreasing trend in both configurations at low Bond numbers, but the two behaviours diverge for ($Bo \gtrsim 0.15$). For the PB regime, when the wall extends from the injector, $St$ approaches an approximately constant value of $St\simeq0.014$, whereas substantially lower values are measured for the downstream-wall configuration. The larger rebound amplitudes and longer return stages observed in the latter configuration account for lower frequencies. These findings indicate that the bubble dynamics depend not only on the conventional control parameters and wall separation, but also on the wall geometry and the associated pre-interaction bubble hydrodynamic history.

</details>


### [132] [Bubble growth on arrays of micro-electrodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24286)
*Mengyuan Huang,Gerd Mutschke,Zhicheng Yuan,Kerstin Eckert*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过三维VOF相变模拟，研究了水电解过程中电极气泡生长对电流密度分布和欧姆电阻的影响。发现非均匀电流密度显著影响气泡动力学，孤立气泡与电极岛阵列上的气泡行为不同，外侧气泡会向外漂移延缓电极覆盖，该漂移受相邻气泡驱动的液体平流控制，与间距1/d²成比例，并受电流密度不对称性调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型假设电极润湿区域电流密度均匀，忽略了气泡几何和演化对电流分布的反作用。本文旨在通过解析一次电场，揭示非均匀电流密度与气泡生长的耦合机制。

Method: 采用Basilisk中的三维几何VOF方法模拟相变过程，解析一次电场，研究单气泡在不同尺寸电极上的生长以及电极岛阵列上多气泡的生长。

Result: 非均匀电流密度显著影响气泡生长动力学；电极岛阵列上的外侧气泡向外漂移，延缓电极全覆盖；漂移由相邻气泡生长驱动的液体平流主导，与间距1/d²成比例，受电流密度不对称调制。

Conclusion: 电极图案化和气泡间距可用于调控电场分布，降低水电解过程中气泡引起的电阻损耗。

Abstract: Gas bubbles evolving on electrodes during water-electrolysis are blocking active reaction area, thus hindering mass transfer and raising Ohmic resistance. Unlike earlier models that prescribe a uniform current density on the wetted part of the electrode, we resolve the primary electric field, which allows the current density and the interfacial gas production to respond to the geometry of the electrode and the temporal evolution of the bubbles. Using three-dimensional geometrical volume-of-fluid (VOF) simulations with phase change in Basilisk, we examine the growth of single-bubbles on electrodes of different size and of multiple bubbles growing on arrays of catalytic electrode islands. The non-uniform current density and the associated Ohmic resistance significantly affect the growth dynamics. Unlike the case of a single bubble, the outer bubbles in case of electrode islands tend to drift outward during growth, thus delaying full electrode coverage and sustaining current. Footprint tracking and a theoretical analysis show that this drift is governed by the liquid advection driven by the growth of neighboring bubbles, scaling with their separation 1/d2, and modulated by the current-density asymmetry. These results show how electrode patterning and bubble spacing can be exploited to tailor the electric field distribution and reduce bubble-induced resistive losses during water electrolysis.

</details>


### [133] [The balance between compactness and forecast accuracy of data-driven latent-space reduced-order models in controlled wake flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24569)
*Alberto Solera-Rico,Patricia García-Caspueñas,Carlos Sanmiguel Vila,Stefano Discetti*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了在主动流动控制中，不同空间编码器（POD、卷积自编码器、变分自编码器）对尾迹流潜空间动力学可预测性的影响，发现POD虽压缩效率低但产生更平滑的潜轨迹，从而获得更稳定的长期预测；CAE虽压缩效率高但潜动力学更不规则，导致长期预测退化更快。揭示了压缩效率与预测精度之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的主动流动控制需要准确、稳定且足够快速的预测模型，现有方法通过降阶模型先压缩高维速度快照到潜空间，再学习潜空间动力学的时间步进预测器。然而，空间编码器的选择如何影响潜坐标的可预测性尚不明确，这对实时优化控制至关重要。

Method: 使用两种受控二维尾流构型（简化卡车尾流和流体弹球），对比POD、非线性卷积自编码器（CAE）和两种变分自编码器的压缩效果，并评估基于LSTM网络的多种时间预测器。

Result: CAE实现更高的压缩效率和更锐利的短期重建，但产生更不规则、频谱更宽的潜动力学，导致长期预测退化更快且灾难性发散概率更高；POD产生更平滑的潜轨迹，更易学习和外推，在短期以上的预测中更可靠。

Conclusion: 存在压缩效率与预测精度之间的明确权衡，潜动力学预测的稳定性比最大化压缩更重要，这对依赖动力学预测的控制策略（如模型预测控制和强化学习）尤为重要，为设计对驱动感知、硬件可行的实时流动控制降阶模型提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Model-based active flow control requires predictive models that are accurate, stable, and fast enough for real-time optimisation. In controlled wake flows, this is often achieved through Reduced-Order Models (ROMs) that first compress high-dimensional velocity snapshots into a latent space and then learn a time- stepping predictor for the dynamics in the latent space. Here, we study how the choice of the spatial encoder affects the predictability of the resulting latent coordinates for wake flows under control inputs. Using two actuated 2D wake configurations, a simplified truck wake and the fluidic pinball, we compare Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) against nonlinear Convolutional Autoencoders (CAEs) and two types of variational autoencoders for compression, and evaluate several temporal predictors based on Long Short-Term Memory networks. CAEs achieve higher compression efficiency and sharper short-term reconstructions, but they produce latent dynamics that are more irregular and with broadband spectral content. As a consequence, long-horizon forecasts degrade faster and show a higher probability of catastrophic divergence than POD-based models. POD yields smoother latent trajectories that are easier to learn and extrapolate, leading to more reliable predictions beyond the short- term regime. These results reveal a clear trade-off between compactness and forecast accuracy, and suggest that the stability of the latent dynamics prediction can outweigh maximal compression. This is particularly relevant for control strategies rooted in forecasts of the dynamics, such as model predictive control and reinforcement learning. The findings provide practical guidance for designing actuation-aware, hardware-feasible predictive ROMs for real-time flow control.

</details>


### [134] [A Modified Moving Reference Frame Method for Propeller Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24630)
*Denis Andreev,Georgios Bletsos,Antonios Kritikos,Niklas Kühl,Thomas Rung*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的移动参考系(mMRF)方法，通过在参考系旋转速率中引入平滑衰减的空间函数，解决了部分旋转网格方法中旋转域与静止域界面处的速度不连续性，在保持与MRF相同计算成本的同时显著减少了界面伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 螺旋桨-船体相互作用的高精度解析对船舶CFD自航点预测至关重要，但滑动界面(SI)方法计算昂贵，经典MRF方法无法捕捉非定常相互作用效应，而部分旋转网格方法虽能折衷但存在旋转域与静止域界面速度不连续的问题。

Method: 提出修改的MRF(mMRF)公式：将参考系旋转速率乘以一个从螺旋桨附近(值为1)到域界面(值为0)平滑衰减的空间函数，从而恢复界面的速度和压力连续性。该公式在RANS求解器FreSCo⁺中实现，通过Taylor-Couette解析解验证，并应用于敞水螺旋桨和JBC自航仿真。

Result: MRF和mMRF均能准确复现主要积分推进量(n, K_T, K_Q, 1-t, 1-w_T, η_R)，但mMRF显著减少了界面不连续性和局部流场中的非物理伪影，尤其是在大MRF分数下，且计算成本基本不变。

Conclusion: mMRF方法能在不增加计算成本的前提下有效消除部分旋转网格方法的界面不连续性，是一种兼顾精度与效率的螺旋桨-船体相互作用模拟方案。

Abstract: Accurate resolution of propeller-hull interaction is essential for predicting the self-propulsion point in ship CFD, yet motion-resolving methods such as sliding interfaces (SI) are computationally expensive, while the classical Moving Reference Frame (MRF) approach cannot capture unsteady interaction effects. Partially rotating grid methods bridge this gap by splitting the propeller rotation into a grid-resolved and an MRF component, but the abrupt transition between the rotating and stationary domains introduces discontinuities in the velocity field. This work presents a modified MRF (mMRF) formulation in which the reference-frame rotation rate is scaled by a spatially varying function that decays smoothly from unity near the propeller to zero at the domain interface, restoring velocity and pressure continuity across the boundary. The governing equations are derived and implemented in the RANS solver FreSCo$^+$, verified against the analytical Taylor--Couette solution, and applied to open-water propeller and Japan Bulk Carrier self-propulsion simulations at model scale. Both MRF and mMRF reproduce the principal integral propulsion quantities ($n$, $K_{\mathrm{T}}$, $K_{\mathrm{Q}}$, $1-t$, $1-w_{\mathrm{T}}$, $η_{\mathrm{R}}$) accurately, but the mMRF markedly reduces interface discontinuities and non-physical artifacts in the local flow field, particularly at large MRF fractions, at essentially the same computational cost.

</details>
